Provider-managed third-party in-site SDK remains disabled; official upstream vendor docs/source/SRI, wallet behavior and a tiny owner-funded on-chain receipt are NOT verified. **NEXT:** verify trustworthy current first-party embeddable SDK artifact and API; then scoped fee-OFF integration PR, staging and minimal owner-wallet mainnet acceptance, independently checked finality/amounts, and explicit production enabling. No Game, ClimbX or Launchpad edits. The latest dated log entry at the bottom is authoritative over older historical checklist items.\n\n## How to resume after a chat ends

1. **Read this file FIRST**, then fetch current `main`, open PRs and exact-head GitHub Actions runs. Do **not** assume the baseline SHA above is still current.
2. Work on **DEX and Swap only** (Launchpad paused). Avoid `game/`, Game assets, `GAME_CONCEPT.md`, gameplay-specific workflows and Game progress records. The other developer handles those.
3. Before every substantial change, inspect the relevant source + current remote CI. Use a focused branch/PR, preserve unrelated changes, and validate exact-head checks before merging. Shared frontend/workflows require extra conflict care.
4. **Update this same record after each meaningful completed task, test result, merge or blocker diagnosis.** Add the date, affected files, branch/PR/commit, exact run URLs and pass/fail conclusion, and one concrete next step. Refresh obsolete statuses; do not append unsupported success claims.
5. No asynchronous work is implied by this document. A future chat resumes by reading it and taking the next step, rather than repeating earlier planning.

### Safety boundaries (non-negotiable)

- Keep `swap-config.js` `enabled: false`, `EXECUTION_ENABLED = "false"`, `SIGNABLE_ORDER_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE = false`, `EXECUTION_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE = false` until a separately documented, independently reviewed and explicitly approved release; no temporary bypass to meet a deadline.
- Quote-only endpoint must never return a wallet-signable order, sign a transaction, or submit an on-chain trade. Production acceptance probes must not call the signable-order creation endpoint. A test/CI green status is not equivalent to verified mainnet execution.
- No private keys, seed phrases, Jupiter keys, RPC secrets or Cloudflare credentials in repo, frontend, logs, or chat.
- Launchpad must remain preview-only until program/SDK provenance, configuration, economics, creator authentication, Devnet launch-to-migration reconciliation, incident controls and independent security review all pass.
- Prefer free/low-cost infrastructure **without downgrading** authentication, validation, key management, rate limiting or asset safety.

## Change log

### 2026-11-14 — Exact DEX/Swap quote inputs merged (PR #300)
- **Scope:** DEX and Swap frontend quote/request arithmetic. No changes to Game, CLIMBX, wallet-key management, live execution gates or Launchpad code.
- **Before:** `main` `ed484fc73a5384cc69ae843371b8936506b07af7`. Both DEX and Swap used `Math.floor(Number(amount) * 10^decimals)`, which can silently undercount (e.g., `0.0000157 SOL` at 9 decimals could become 15,699 rather than 15,700 raw units; `0.0157` at 6 decimals could become 15,699 rather than 15,700). Exact amounts are a requirement for a trustworthy quote.
- **Changed:** new `dex/swap-amount.js` decimal-string-to-`BigInt` u64 converter and `.github/scripts/swap-amount.test.mjs`; updated `dex/index.html` and `swap/index.html` quote and disabled future order preparation paths; updated `.github/workflows/browser-smoke.yml` with unit and real-button browser checks. Branch `fix/swap-exact-raw-token-amounts`, [PR #300](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/300), merged as `4d7de3c199b01d197d9d83f5050c46614e7efa00`.
- **Verified:** exact-head [Validate Climboria run 43465254482](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43465254482) **SUCCESS**; [Browser Smoke run 43465254493](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43465254493) **SUCCESS**, testing 15,700 precise raw units for 0.0000157 SOL, fractional/overflow rejection, DEX and Swap quote rendering. [GitHub Pages run 43465518518](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43465518518) **SUCCESS**; post-merge Validate Climboria and Browser Smoke on commit `4d7de3c` also **SUCCESS**.
- **Unresolved:** quote-only acceptance does not establish safe mainnet execution; signing and execution remain gated off, Launchpad preview-only. Post-deploy workflow sequencing and physical mobile / real-origin browser quote acceptance still need independent evidence.
- **NEXT:** confirm exact post-merge Pages/Browser status for `4d7de3c`, then verify real quote-only UX on deployed DEX and Swap, provider-error states, and small-amount behavior; do not enable signing.


### 2026-11-14 — DEX quote race/inconsistent-input fix merged (PR #298)

No assertions about stale responses or incorrect amounts were dropped.
- **Verified:** exact-head `b75fedc36a556932ebed83b49bc9a6eba5e5e670` [Validate 43463736814](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43463736814) **SUCCESS** and [Browser Smoke 43463738066](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43463738066) **SUCCESS**. Production Pages post-merge check [run 43463998989](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43463998989) **SUCCESS**.
- **Unresolved:** physical mobile-device acceptance, longer-term API availability, and the new `workflow_run` sequencing on a future Worker deployment are unverified. All real-money execution gates stay disabled; Launchpad remains preview-only.

```text
YYYY-MM-DD — <specific task>
Scope: DEX | Swap | Launchpad
Before: <main SHA / PR head and observed problem>
Changed: <files, branch, PR, commit>
Verified: <exact GitHub Actions run URLs + conclusion; production-origin evidence if any>
Unresolved: <remaining blockers / risks>
NEXT: <single specific next coding or verification action>
```

**Cross-chat ownership:** Game owner must independently keep a separate `docs/GAME_DEVELOPMENT_RECORD.md`. Neither chat should alter the other chat's progress record. If a change to shared code is unavoidable, communicate the exact file, branch and PR before touching it.

Both clusters read successfully from finalized RPC; official source hashes matched pins; 13 pinned SDK builder discriminators and live critical instruction discriminators matched; checked GlobalConfig/PlatformConfig SDK account lengths, discriminators and layout sizes were binary-compatible. **Critical account TYPE SCHEMAS DID NOT match published pinned IDL on EITHER network**:
- `GlobalConfig`: pinned published IDL includes `requires_platform_auth: u8` and 7-byte padding, while legacy live on-chain IDL describes 8-byte alignment padding without that explicit field.
- `PlatformConfig`: pinned IDL has older padding/curve-params representation; live Anchor IDL includes `restrict_global_config`, `restrict_curve_param`, `curve_rule_manager` and differently sized padding.
- `BondingCurveParam`, `PlatformCurveParam`: types present in pinned repo IDL but missing from the legacy live IDL.
- `PlatformConfigParam`: type layout differs. This drift is identical in both Mainnet and Devnet live reports.
Root cause classification: **historical pinned published SDK/IDL vs deployed legacy Anchor-IDL schema-version drift**, not an RPC outage or signature mismatch. Do not infer which source best reflects current bytecode merely from equality of account lengths or instruction discriminators. Do not remove mismatching checks, claim full SDK compatibility or unlock create/migrate.
Unresolved: obtain and independently hash-review **current** official Raydium SDK/IDL releases and source commit, reconcile field definitions and post-2026 SDK migration semantics against deployed bytecode and real accounts, review fee/LP custody and program authority, Devnet creator create→trade→CPMM migration→LP-lock lifecycle, independent semantic security review. **Mainnet Launchpad and Swap financial execution remain DISABLED**.

Five confirmed **SDK static vs official pinned published IDL writable-flag mismatches** are *all* in the SDK `claimPlatformFee` builder: index 3 `platform_config`, 6 `quote_mint`, 7 `token_program`, 8 `system_program` and 9 `associated_token_program`: SDK says `isWritable:true`, pinned IDL omits writable (false). No false assertion about whether Solana deployed bytecode accepts these excess writable privileges or whether other constraints are safe. Unresolved: SDK/pinned official IDL writable-flag discrepancies above, 9 runtime token-program address bindings, dynamic appended accounts, and prior 11/5 **pinned-vs-live legacy Anchor IDL** drift. No proof of SDK-produced transaction metas, deployed-program behavior, LP-lock recipient or custody, upgrade authority safety, Devnet creator lifecycle, or independent audit. **Mainnet Launchpad and signed Swap/DEX execution remain DISABLED**. A green source review workflow does not certify release safety.
NEXT: reconcile the five `claimPlatformFee` SDK-vs-official IDL flag differences against deployed instruction semantics and test strictly read-only exact account-meta snapshots for the callsites (including how `mintProgramB`/`tokenProgramB` are chosen from verified mint owner). ### 2026-11-14 — Swap Jupiter quote optional provider metadata sanitization merged

### 2026-11-14 — Swap/DEX stale quote state, network isolation and optional quote display fixed
Scope: **ONLY /swap/, /dex/ quote-preview UI, matching quote test fixtures and isolated GitHub CI**. No Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, wallet permissions, API order/execute logic, SPL token creation, financial transfers or execution-gate toggles.
Before: main baseline `410abeca5362fdd7716848389b2719869f3f4947`. Previous [PR #340](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/340) sanitized optional upstream Jupiter quote metadata at the Worker, but the browser UI still displayed `null bps` for explicitly null provider fees and could format malformed fallback impact as `NaN%`. On Swap, selecting Base during an in-flight Solana quote reset the panel without invalidating its request sequence, so a delayed Solana success could repopulate an unsupported Base quote. Changing slippage also left old quote information valid, and a changed amount during an in-flight request could leave the Get quote button disabled because prior `finally` only restored the previous sequence.
Changed: [PR #343](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/343) merged as `a212c8229fb682ce245c379a3af83f5c35d34638`. Scoped changes: `swap/index.html`, `dex/index.html`, the existing Swap amount-test VM fixture, a new `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.mjs`, and an isolated `.github/workflows/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.yml`. Swap chain changes now invalidate outstanding quotes, cancel pending auto-quote, never autoquote on Base, preserve the Base disabled quote button and compare exact source amount text, chain, mints, decimals, and selected slippage on response/error. Quote invalidation also restores the Get quote button instead of stranding it in `Quoting…`. DEX now binds slippage to the pending request and invalidates the quote on slippage and Auto Route preference changes. Both pages display `Not supplied` for missing/malformed fee or impact rather than 0, `null bps`, `NaN%`, or an object router label. All original exact provider raw input/output matching, non-signable quote-only status and execution kill-switch checks remain enforced.
Initial verification failures and fixes: [PR job 43505226356](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43505226356) initially **FAILED** on a case-sensitive fixture label check: the real browser CSS renders `ESTIMATED OUTPUT` uppercase. [PR run 43505378301](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43505378301) then **FAILED** on fixture expectation `Base network selected` whereas the actual quote-status text is `Base ERC-20 swap is not yet connected`; corrected fixture expectation only. [existing browser smoke 43505378123](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43505378123) also failed because its isolated quote-handler VM fixture lacked the newly required `selectedChain:'solana'`, corrected fixture without weakening the production chain check. ### 2026-11-14 — Amount/token ABA stale quotes and DEX mint-RPC selection race blocked
Scope: **DEX read-only market/quote state and existing Swap/DEX desktop/mobile regression tests only.** No Game, ClimbX, swap proxy order/execute backend, wallet signing, Launchpad minting, program allowlist or release flags changed.
Before: latest `main` at kickoff `bd7e6de17eb7cf4eb67bee42eafe41828d30d861`. Existing Swap/DEX quote handlers bound one in-flight response to a monotonically invalidated request ID, selected mint IDs, amount and slippage; but previous CI did not exercise **A→B→A** amount/token transitions. The DEX token-detail terminal also had a separate bug: `dexTradeSyncToken` could accept a stale earlier `getTokenSupply` response after a newer market was selected and assign the old mint's decimals to the new token. Once a valid mint response arrived, previous code could re-enable `dexTradeGetQuote` and `dexTradePrepare` despite the DEX terminal's deliberately disabled controls.
Changed: [PR #345](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/345) merged as `e1d79445c3c3592d2c24b0445e742259900c1309`. Only `dex/index.html`, `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.mjs`, new `.github/scripts/dex-trade-rpc-race.test.mjs`, and `.github/workflows/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.yml` changed. The DEX market selection now immediately invalidates the old read-only quote. Solana mint-supply RPC completion/error is strictly tied to the latest market selection sequence; older successes and errors cannot mutate newer mint decimals, terminal status or quote mode, or run stale RPC fallback requests. Existing quote logic remains bound to input amounts, selected mints and user actions. Production Buy/Sell and Market/Limit terminal controls remain deliberately **disabled** and are never enabled just because a mint is verified; new explicit `DEX_TRADE_TERMINAL_INTERACTION_ENABLED=false` guards both quote and prepare controls in mint success and order-mode update paths. The independent visible Jupiter read-only quote preview remains available. The separate trade-control script signals quote invalidation to the separate preview script through a narrow internal UI event; this event never creates orders or transactions.
Verification details: initial new browser test [43506985266](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43506985266) failed because the fixture attempted to directly call `chooseQuoteToken` outside its private DEX script scope; fixed by exercising the existing delegated token-selection click handler instead, without exposing global internals. Intermediate new browser [43507457400](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43507457400) and [43507914530](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43507914530) caught the truly disabled trading tabs: production disabled buttons cannot be clicked. Kept them disabled, changed tests to temporarily enable only their local fixture, click to verify latent invalidation, and restore disabled state. All initial red findings were investigated/fixed rather than bypassed.
Exact PR head `bf9e4a98f801b6642b5638e0f1a533f6845525c9` passed [Validate 43508353916](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43508353916), [full Browser Smoke 43508353597](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43508353597), and [read-only Quote State CI 43508353448](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43508353448): all **SUCCESS**. Quote-state CI logged **28 desktop/mobile Playwright PASS scenarios**, including amount A→B→A and token A→B→A, changed token-search input, missing fee/impact, Base network switch, slippage, preview close, and synthetic dormant mode-switch handlers. Node VM also logged **4 PASS isolated mint-RPC race tests**, including old result arrives before/after new selection, obsolete transport failures, and invalid latest decimals. ### 2026-11-14 — Jupiter V6 route-plan topology: reject disconnected and abandoned intermediate branches
Scope: **ONLY** `dex/swap-proxy/src/jupiter-v6-policy.mjs`, `dex/swap-proxy/test/jupiter-v6-policy.test.mjs`, and this DEX/Swap/Launchpad handoff record. No Game, ClimbX, Auth, Launchpad runtime, wallet signing, order store, Cloudflare deployment, or release-gate code changed.

Finding: The existing Jupiter V6 route decoder enforced one input token index 0, an edge to the final output token index equal to the step count, increasing hop indices, and per-input 100% split totals. This was insufficient to prove that **every route edge** is on a path from input to final output. Two concrete malformed examples that previously passed those structural checks: (a) `0→3:100%; 1→2:100%; 2→3:100%` (index 1 never produced); (b) `0→1:25%; 0→3:35%; 0→3:40%` (25% stranded at an unconsumed intermediate index). The gate is dormant and transaction execution remains hard-disabled, so do **not** assert these were live exploitable vulnerabilities.

Implementation: [PR #348](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/348) merged into main as `9d7284f0a6c0d80e08217fb3fab8be3843e58579`. Added conservative route-graph checks: each nonzero input index must have a producing step; each nonfinal output index must be consumed by a subsequent step. Together with strict increasing token indexes, all accepted intermediate edges are sourced and terminate. Valid two-hop and source-split connected plans still pass. Added raw binary negative fixtures for unreachable inputs and orphaned intermediate outputs through both decoder and parsed transaction validation. This **does not** independently prove Jupiter V6 CPI execution, per-DEX program account roles, token movements, mint and custody recipients, fee destinations, ALT privileges, or account substitution resistance. Supported programs and account privileges were not expanded.

Exact PR head `5cfbde19e06c32ece2263a17552800d6dffedfc3`: [Validate 43512327758](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512327758) **SUCCESS**, **80/80** Worker/security cases passed with Wrangler dry-run; [Browser Smoke 43512327652](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512327652) **SUCCESS**. Exact merged SHA: [main Validate 43512500646](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512500646) **SUCCESS**, [Browser Smoke 43512500655](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512500655) **SUCCESS**, [GitHub Pages 43512500211](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512500211) **SUCCESS**. [Production read-only quote smoke 43512500650](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43512500650) **SUCCESS** (job `132186093942`): CI verified exact `9d7284f0a6c0d80e08217fb3fab8be3843e58579` Pages deployment first, then tested live Jupiter proxy quote-only SOL/USDC with exact raw units on DEX and Swap desktop/mobile; no wallet transaction. DexScreener market response and DEX close/reopen quote reset also passed; this is NOT on-chain pool/chart validation or Worker deployment proof.

Open: **Swap wallet-signable order and execution remain source-level disabled**; Jupiter DEX CPI account-role, fee recipient and token-movement semantics still require independent bytecode/source review and controlled Devnet lifecycle. ### 2026-11-14 — Jupiter V6 resolved account aliases / program writable privilege (fail-closed)
Scope: ONLY `dex/swap-proxy/src/jupiter-v6-policy.mjs` and `dex/swap-proxy/test/jupiter-v6-policy.test.mjs` (plus this DEX/Swap/Launchpad handoff record). No Game, ClimbX, Auth, Launchpad, public swap frontend, Cloudflare deployment or release-gate change.

Finding and fix: The existing Jupiter V6 policy reconstructed Solana v0 static + ALT-loaded key arrays, but when checking the DEX program ID in the remaining instruction accounts it used the first matching account index. Malformed duplicated resolved public-key aliases could let an apparently readonly first instance mask an additional writable alias. Also program-id privilege checks were limited to the route's referenced fixed/remaining accounts; a known executable program might be writable elsewhere in the resolved account keyspace. These findings are **conditional structural gaps in a disabled signable-order path**, NOT proven exploits of the live quote-only deployment.

[PR #351](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/351) merged as `edcd429c3360f0596c10685627eb64d29b7703e5`. New conservative, fail-closed rules reject **any duplicate resolved public keys** across static, ALT writable and ALT readonly lists. The policy checks **every** resolved known executable program pubkey (Jupiter V6, SPL Token, Compute Budget and supported DEX program IDs) for unexpected signer/writable privileges, including unused or loaded keys. Repeated **instruction references to the same program ID sentinel index remain allowed**, as distinct account references are not duplicated resolved key entries. No previously unsupported program or instruction family is permitted. The normal readonly DEX program passed by one unique ALT lookup still passes the structural validator.

Adversarial fixtures added for duplicated source/destination, duplicate static DEX program alias, static-vs-ALT and writable-vs-readonly ALT aliases, unused known DEX program illegally writable via ALT, referenced DEX program writable through ALT, and out-of-range account indexes. Positive readonly unique ALT program fixture added. Older negative tests previously created duplicate keys as a side effect while trying to test wrong taker/mint and missing program; the first PR-head Validate [43514173643](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514173643) correctly failed **three stale error-message expectations**, not an execution regression. Updated the old fixtures to use unique incorrect keys and added a dedicated writable output-mint ALT case without weakening the validator. Exact final PR head `b52a7aaef3c7b3ebd7991ad1bc2e630f150930d9`: [Validate 43514341559](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514341559) **SUCCESS (84/84 Worker/security tests and Wrangler dry-run)** and [Browser Smoke 43514341567](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514341567) **SUCCESS**.

Exact post-merge `edcd429c3360f0596c10685627eb64d29b7703e5`: [Validate 43514489199](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514489199) **SUCCESS**; [Browser Smoke 43514489284](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514489284) **SUCCESS**, [Pages 43514489452](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514489452) **SUCCESS**, and [Production Read-only Jupiter Browser 43514489375](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43514489375) **SUCCESS** (production job `132193035061`). Browser workflow verified exact GitHub Pages deployment of this merged SHA BEFORE testing real Jupiter SOL/USDC read-only quotes on Swap and DEX desktop/mobile. All quote flows returned expected raw units without transactions; real DexScreener market response and DEX preview close/reopen were also verified. This confirms only the deployed quote-only UI, not a Cloudflare Worker code deploy, on-chain CPI correctness or live swaps.

Open critical security blockers (unchanged): Token movement / CPI account-role / fee destination semantics for all allowed DEX variants are not independently proven; finalized source/destination token-account reads and generic privilege checks do not replace per-DEX CPI schemata. No controlled actual Devnet wallet-sign → Worker-verify → submit → chain-confirm → balance-reconcile lifecycle has been accepted. Independent Solana security review, production Worker infrastructure provenance and monitoring remain additional gates. Raydium Launchpad remains blocked on deployed IDL account/type divergences and CPMM mint-program, LP custody and fee-recipient semantics. **Worker flags `EXECUTION_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE=false` and `SIGNABLE_ORDER_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE=false`, Wrangler `EXECUTION_ENABLED=false` and public frontend `enabled:false` remain mandatory.** This does not authorize signing, trading or token minting.

### 2026-11-14 — Jupiter V6 classic SPL Token-only program guard + taker fee-payer binding + unique Compute Budget fields
Scope: **DEX/Swap Worker policy ONLY**: `dex/swap-proxy/src/jupiter-v6-policy.mjs`, its deterministic `dex/swap-proxy/test/jupiter-v6-policy.test.mjs`, and this workstream's record/handoff docs. No Game, ClimbX, Auth, Launchpad runtime, DEX/Swap public frontend, or security release switches modified.

Finding: the existing policy checked fixed Jupiter route token-program slot for classic SPL Token, and the RPC verifier checked classic user source/destination token accounts, but the policy did **not** reject a Token-2022 executable program key among extra remaining accounts, unreferenced static keys or writable/readonly ALT-loaded keys. A route could therefore carry an unsupported token program for potential intermediate CPI despite outer token-account checks. Also, the Durable Object independently binds the order fee payer to taker, but the route-policy function previously did not check this itself. The Compute Budget handler accepted repeated valid setting opcodes (2 limit, 3 priority-price, 4 loaded-account-data size), which are ambiguous/invalid transaction semantics. The signable path was and remains source-disabled: **no claim of active exploitability**.

Fix: [PR #354](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/354) merged as `fc0f59c467a73b3913192eb9c9811833ad0a8dd0`.
- Reject canonical `TokenzQdBNbLqP5VEhdkAS6EPFLC1PHnBqCXEpPxuEb` (Token-2022) **anywhere** in resolved static + ALT key arrays, including when used only as a route remaining account. This excludes one known unsupported program but is **not** full CPI semantic validation or independent on-chain program proof.
- Require static v0 fee-payer account at index 0 to match requested taker; this is defense in depth in addition to the already existing order-store proof.
- Reject repeated Compute Budget setting types, allowing legitimate distinct ComputeUnitLimit, ComputeUnitPrice and LoadedAccountsDataSizeLimit settings once each. The supported instruction/DEX variant set was **not** expanded.
- Offline negative fixtures: wrong fee payer, Token-2022 in unused static readonly, referenced remaining readonly, ALT readonly and writable, duplicate unit limit/priority price/loaded-data-size. Positive fixture: three distinct valid Compute Budget fields. Existing wrong transfer-authority test now preserves correct fee payer to independently prove the route-role rejection.

Exact final PR head `12e6f4b617492e5c79e53f3353b9d5b91a4e8902`: [Validate 43515829972](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515829972) **SUCCESS** including **88/88** Worker/security cases and Wrangler dry-run, and [Browser Smoke 43515830118](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515830118) **SUCCESS**. Merged main SHA above; separate main post-merge [Validate 43515995331](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515995331) **SUCCESS**, [Browser Smoke 43515995619](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515995619) **SUCCESS**, [Pages 43515994715](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515994715) **SUCCESS**, and [production real-Jupiter quote smoke 43515995261](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43515995261) **SUCCESS** (production verification job `132198062618`). Browser workflow verified the exact `fc0f59c467a73b3913192eb9c9811833ad0a8dd0` Pages deployment before testing read-only SOL/USDC Jupiter quote via proxy on Swap and DEX at desktop/mobile sizes, exact raw units with **no transaction**, DexScreener market response and DEX quote discard on preview close/reopen. This is a browser-based production-origin quote-only check, **not** proof of deployed Worker runtime SHA, signed swap safety, full DEX pool/chart integrity or actual on-chain execution.

**Unresolved security gates remain critical:** source-level `EXECUTION_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE=false`, `SIGNABLE_ORDER_SECURITY_REVIEW_COMPLETE=false`, Wrangler `EXECUTION_ENABLED="false"`, frontend `enabled:false`; no wallet-signable order or execute. Real CPI account-role, vault/authority/mint/fee destination and inner token-movement semantics for every approved DEX variant remain unproven; controlled Devnet wallet-sign → Worker verify → Jupiter submit → chain confirm → balance reconcile and independent Solana review not accepted. Launchpad Raydium deployed IDL account/type inconsistencies, dynamic mint-program binding, CPMM fee/LP custody and creator end-to-end Devnet lifecycle remain separate unresolved blockers.

NEXT: pursue **one DEX variant at a time** with independent pinned account-role/CPI/token-movement evidence and adversarial fee-recipient, vault, authority, token-mint and signer/writable fixtures, rejecting unrecognized shapes. Inspect Launchpad CPMM LP destination/fee recipient via separate readonly workstream. Do not reopen execution gates or conflate quote-only production verification with successful signed trading.

### 2026-11-14 — Independent Raydium V4 authority PDA derivation; PR #364 MERGED
**Scope: DEX/Swap security ONLY.** No Game, ClimbX, homepage, Auth, Launchpad runtime, cloud execution deployment, frontend trading flag or on-chain wallet transaction modified.

**Finding:** The earlier read-only Raydium pool snapshot inspector matched classic SPL vault owners to the *caller-supplied* expectedAuthority, without independently deriving the AMM authority. A forged but internally consistent caller claim could therefore satisfy that structural check. This was a read-only forensic trust gap, **not an observed exploitable live swap** (actual signing and execution were already source-disabled).

**[PR #364](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/364), merged as bd86399a62f9420115027766f931396331ea6693.** The new standalone src/raydium-v4-authority-pda.mjs pins Raydium authority derivation to [AUTHORITY_AMM seed](https://github.com/raydium-io/raydium-amm/blob/91739d8d9f5cf347e0ce14ebe4d2bb7069fb31b6/program/src/processor.rs#L96) and [create_program_address nonce formula](https://github.com/raydium-io/raydium-amm/blob/91739d8d9f5cf347e0ce14ebe4d2bb7069fb31b6/program/src/processor.rs#L284-L290) at source commit 91739d8d9f5cf347e0ce14ebe4d2bb7069fb31b6. It derives SHA256("amm authority" + u8 nonce + 32-byte Raydium program ID + "ProgramDerivedAddress") via WebCrypto; conservative Edwards25519 decompression rejects ambiguous/on-curve candidates. Test vectors: mainnet nonce 254 derives 5Q544fKrFoe6tsEbD7S8EmxGTJYAKtTVhAW5Q5pge4j1; Devnet nonce 254 derives AhSQwzpJMskCCQ3GkWDZSA69xS5qK5s7jLejhK6sgYf1. Nonce 255 rejects on both networks.

The async read-only fetchRaydiumV4FinalizedPoolEvidence path now derives the PDA from the fetched 752-byte AMM state nonce and the network-specific Raydium program ID, then requires the supplied authority and both raw vault token owners to match. The synchronous offline inspector still reports ammAuthorityPdaIndependentlyVerified:false; only the async PDA-checked path may report true. The evidence module is **not imported by handleOrder or handleExecute**, and every result retains approvedForJupiterCpi:false. Negative tests include a forged-but-consistent caller authority, incorrect on-curve nonce, wrong network, non-integer nonce, and separate static/ALT or route validation regression tests.

### 2026-11-14 — PR #403 development checkpoint: bounded Durable Object JSON and safe failures

**Branch:** `security/swap-order-store-bounded-requests-20261114`, based on main at PR #402 merge `1d1f89381533f3a445b9b3cd1c415b644a7ed4c0`. Code commit `fef3fdffea3bbe916ddb48bf37d34f07a27e7a1b`, test commit `9f51b99598b47e6fd96dbe6a3dd77e5d476da045`. **Only Swap Durable Object and its tests changed**, no Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, Auth, homepage, frontend quote UI, API route contract, source-level signing/execute kill switches or wallet keys.

**Concrete risk and fix:** `dex/swap-proxy/src/order-store.mjs` previously called `request.text()` on internal `/store`, `/claim` and `/settle` before enforcing its 60,000-character limit. Future signable order/execute expansion would expose uncontrolled memory allocation to an attacker, including oversized malformed JSON and JSONP. Replace it with the existing well-tested `readBoundedRequestJson(request, 60000)` to enforce a true **60,000 BYTE** limit during streaming, reject oversized advertised Content-Length before reading, reject invalid/missing JSON media type and invalid UTF-8, cancel immediately on overage, and suppress raw storage/runtime exception text. Return fixed 400 for malformed payloads, fixed 503 for internal storage/crypto exceptions. No change to order transaction binding, atomic one-time claim, expiry or settlement semantics.

**Verification so far:** new adversarial synthetic streaming tests cover all three DO routes, oversized advertised length, body-stream overflow and cancellation, content-type smuggling, invalid UTF-8, exception-secret suppression, valid streaming JSON, fixed internal failure and removal of unbounded `request.text()`. **CI and PR status PENDING** at the time of this checkpoint: never label these tests passed until exact-head GitHub Actions confirms.

**NEXT exact:** open focused PR #403 (or update this paragraph with actual assigned PR if different), inspect DEX CPI security, Validate, Browser checks, fix failures rather than weakening tests, merge if all green, and verify real quote-only Worker main deployment, Pages and live quote smoke. Record exact tested head/CI URLs/merge SHA and all remaining native-execution blockers. Still no `SOLANA_RPC_URL` private operator configured for Devnet and no independent Jupiter CPI review.

### 2026-11-14 — Swap execution correctness: bind exact claimed signature and forbid provider-only completed status

**Scope strictly Swap/DEX native execution future path.** Previous PRs #401–#406 are MERGED with production **quote-only** accepted; DO NOT repeat those proofs. Baseline `main` prior to this change `8ebfdf603099ac94bf7bf1c72b6c9df815b03cf7`. No Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, Auth, public wallet UX or external API key work.

**Finding:** while source-hard-disabled, the future Worker `handleExecute` incorrectly allowed an upstream Jupiter JSON `Success`, `code:0`, *arbitrary valid-looking signature* to write DO `completed` and report end-user `Success` without any independently verified finalized transaction or matching its Ed25519-authenticated signature to the claimed signed transaction. Also, malformed/oversized provider JSON could escape to generic outer errors rather than explicitly marking outcome unknown after one-time claim.

**Implementation on branch `security/swap-provider-success-not-finality-20261114`:**
- `src/jupiter-execution-outcome-policy.mjs` commit `77bd69283502bdb92ec883771138f587914f5b08`: pure fail-closed provider outcome classification; exact claimed first signature required, provider Success -> ONLY `provider_success_unconfirmed` / `PendingConfirmation`, never on-chain `completed`; contradictions/missing/mismatch -> `uncertain`, legitimate provider failure -> `failed`; all outcomes explicitly `settledOnChain:false`.
- `src/order-store.mjs` commit `60777174e61321a5db1273fd73f7bff9d5ed3cbc`: derive base58 transaction ID from Ed25519-verified signed v0 transaction at atomic `/claim`, persist `claimedSignature` and return it in trusted claim record. `/settle` **rejects `completed`** until a separate audited finalized chain transition exists. Only `provider_success_unconfirmed` plus identical exact claimed signature can be recorded, and even on failed/uncertain any supplied signature must match.
- `src/index.mjs` commit `532c6047b6c0266febc6b0df7a3d283aa4945c87`: future `/execute` requires claimed signature from DO, verifies provider output matches, treats malformed/oversized/transport HTTP outcomes as `uncertain`; returns HTTP 202 `PendingConfirmation`, `confirmation:"unverified"`, `settledOnChain:false` for Jupiter-provider success, never user-facing finalized Success; does not publish unverified execution amounts as settled balances. Source-hard-disabled signing/execute gates unchanged.
- New adversarial `test/jupiter-execution-outcome-policy.test.mjs` commit `632e75721594970e79fc803b4e9265e4d4b00af4`; extended existing `test/order-store.test.mjs` commit `558c48b36eb1e32a4cc85679f8e7bb3d4d4ce714` with matching/mismatched signatures, claimed transaction binding, no fake `completed`, provider pending, uncertain/failure replay locks.
- `docs/DEX_SWAP_EXECUTION_SETTLEMENT_STATE_MACHINE.md` commit `3bd6339db6d91d4447dc903d4a34904e9e5f2771` explains actual chain-finality requirement, missing privileged confirmation endpoint and on-chain acceptance tests.

**CI and PR status**: **PENDING at checkpoint**. Tests are synthetic; no genuine wallet transaction, private Solana RPC, independent Jupiter/Raydium source-to-deployed-bytecode audit or CPI semantic certification. No actual production trades. The first-party quote-only API and DEX/Swap browser currently operational per recorded PR #406 evidence; this branch does NOT authorize real trading.

**NEXT exact:** create focused PR from branch, inspect latest-head DEX Swap CPI Security Node tests and Wrangler dry-run + Validate + Browser Smoke; correct genuine failures, merge after all pass; verify postmerge Worker deployed exact SHA and live Jupiter read-only quote/search plus Pages DEX/Swap real browser smoke. Append exact PR number, tested head/merge SHAs and successful real production run/job IDs. THEN externally acquire trusted RPCs, independent ProgramData pins and CPI audit, obtain real Devnet supported route, and build **audited chain-finality transition** linking `claimedSignature` to the verified receipt and balances after controlled real sign/execute. ### 2026-11-14 — P0 future execution: Jupiter provider failure is NOT on-chain final failure (PR checks pending)

**STRICT scope: Swap/DEX only.** Start from main `f1a468c4417959ef8f252964059e9a3bbe853573`. All PRs #401–#407, #409, #411 and #412 were already merged and last main exact-merge live production quotes accepted. **Do not redo prior PRs; do not touch Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, Auth or source gates.**

**Actual unfixed safety bug found in dormant future Worker execution path:** Jupiter `/execute` may report `{"status":"Failed","code":nonzero}` when an actual signed transaction **has already been broadcast, is unresolved, or can still settle**. The existing status policy treated this off-chain provider report as DO final `failed`, and the Worker returned HTTP 200 `status:"Failed"` to the user—even while `settledOnChain:false`. That misleading terminal label could encourage a second trade/double submission without a finalized signature check. Previous PR #407 correctly prevented provider-only `Success` from becoming `completed`; this branch **closes the symmetric provider-failure gap**.

**Branch** `security/swap-provider-failure-is-unconfirmed-20261114`.
- `dex/swap-proxy/src/jupiter-execution-outcome-policy.mjs`, commit `ac11341f5bd22342f470b72412b3b36f80097301`: a matching/absent-signature nonzero-code Jupiter `Failed` is only `provider_failure_unconfirmed`, public `PendingConfirmation`, `settledOnChain:false`, never final `Failed`. Always surface the **DO Ed25519-verified claimed signed transaction signature** even when Jupiter omitted it; no new signing or submission. Mismatched/contradictory provider payloads remain `uncertain`.
- `dex/swap-proxy/src/order-store.mjs`, commit `bf3ac2655eae04d53c61a665cb72327503e28f12`: internal `/settle` now **rejects both `completed` AND final `failed`** from the unaudited provider-only path. Allows only `provider_success_unconfirmed`, `provider_failure_unconfirmed` and `uncertain` after the one-time claim, and requires the **exact verified claimed signature for both provider-observation states**. None of these unlock replay.
- `dex/swap-proxy/src/index.mjs`, commit `6c5e891cfc3583efcba721a153c6e4c490d33f14`: a matching provider failure receives HTTP **202**, `status:"PendingConfirmation"`, `confirmation:"unverified"`, `settledOnChain:false`, and a clear warning **not to blindly resubmit**; provider success also remains HTTP 202 unconfirmed. No live source gate changed.
- `test/jupiter-execution-outcome-policy.test.mjs` commit `1a86d83f17e66e1927c649a3ca11c62633ccafcb`: synthetic provider failure with claimed, absent, null provider signature always yields pending and authenticated claimed signature, no final state; negative malformed/contradictory responses remain unknown, source gates remain false.
- `test/order-store.test.mjs` commit `8a2404041e2648859c4d5432ef636f46af86712f`: final `failed` rejected HTTP 400 with no state change; missing/mismatched signature cannot settle provider observation; matching claimed signature allows `provider_failure_unconfirmed`; one-time replay rejected, unknown state remains replay-locked.
- `docs/DEX_SWAP_EXECUTION_SETTLEMENT_STATE_MACHINE.md` commit `7967861d98c46ca78009ba4a74b16c19991baae3`: corrected operator/public semantics, no final failed until external reviewed finalized-chain reconciliation, no blind retry.

**No actual onchain execution or provider failure was observed.** All tests are deterministic fixtures. The two Worker source-hard gates, Wrangler `EXECUTION_ENABLED="false"`, frontend `enabled:false` stay OFF; no real wallet signed/submit and production still QUOTE-ONLY. Jupiter CPI, provider authenticity, independently authenticated Solana finality, full deployed source/binary parity, operator private RPCs, controlled Devnet test, secure privileged finalized order-state transition and external security audit remain P0 blockers. **Never enable native trading by changing the status label alone.**

**Important new safety fix (NOT redundant with #407):** future Jupiter `/execute` could previously return `status:"Failed"`, HTTP 200 and write DO final `failed` when Jupiter returned a matching/absent-signature nonzero-code provider error, despite **no finalized on-chain failure proof**. Such a message could mislead a user into placing a duplicate trade while the signed transaction already landed or remained unresolved. #413 changes `classifyJupiterExecutionOutcome` to `provider_failure_unconfirmed`, `PendingConfirmation`, `settledOnChain:false`, with the authentic DO-claimed signed first signature even if Jupiter omitted its returned signature; missing/mismatched/contradictory responses remain `uncertain`. DO `/settle` **rejects both `completed` and final `failed`** on the provider-only path; allowed states are ONLY `provider_success_unconfirmed`, `provider_failure_unconfirmed`, `uncertain`. A provider observation **must include the exact cryptographically claimed signature**. The future Worker returns **HTTP 202** and explicit **do not blindly resubmit** message for provider failure. One-time order replay lock remains; no private RPC, wallet signing, transaction submission, or privileged independent-finality DO transition added. Updated `docs/DEX_SWAP_EXECUTION_SETTLEMENT_STATE_MACHINE.md`, policy and DO signature/replay regression tests.

### 2026-11-14 — PR #420 MERGED; atomic Durable Object claim-safe alarm cleanup, 294/294 tests, exact production quote-only acceptance GREEN

**Scope / concurrent changes preserved:** focused [PR #420](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/420), final tested PR head `c572ca63fa3ea59538de1897716a1a8208605207`, squash merge `fd2728056fdb1049901f226bb1cce38908e5ec69`. Exactly THREE scoped paths changed: `dex/swap-proxy/src/order-store.mjs`, `dex/swap-proxy/test/order-store.test.mjs`, `docs/DEX_SWAP_EXECUTION_SETTLEMENT_STATE_MACHINE.md`. **NO Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, Auth, public frontend, API quote handlers or signing/execution switches touched.** Concurrent [PR #417](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/417) `9b640745625b9c4c5c82246598070973466f04f6` (public Jupiter quote error sanitization) and main handoff checkpoint `89e9d7fc15783ca5af95afeae237f99ea6918eb8` fully preserved; never revert, copy over or redo #417.

**Exact defect closed:** #414 added 30-day claimed order retention, but existing Cloudflare Durable Object `alarm()` first **awaited** `storage.get("order")` and later **awaited** `storage.deleteAll()` if its read said `ready` or expired. At the async gap, a legitimate wallet could Ed25519-sign and atomically `/claim` the order, causing state `executing` and a cryptographically bound first signature, then the stale alarm would delete the now-claimed record and replay/investigation evidence despite promised retention. #420 uses one **synchronous `storage.transactionSync()`** transaction to read the CURRENT order, conditionally delete ONLY the order KV key, or atomically migrate legacy `retainUntil`. If claim wins before cleanup, the claimed record and signature remain; if unclaimed cleanup wins first, later claim sees no order. Preserve 24-hour unclaimed/30-day claimed retention, replay locks for `executing`, `uncertain`, `provider_success_unconfirmed`, `provider_failure_unconfirmed`; reschedule claimed alarm after transaction and throw on scheduling failure rather than delete evidence. Per-key deletion also preserves unrelated durable object KV entries. **No independent blockchain finality or trade authorization implied.**

**Superseded branch handling:** [PR #418](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/418) was CLOSED UNMERGED when concurrent #417 commit created a shared-record conflict. [PR #419](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/419) was CLOSED UNMERGED because a later concurrent #417 docs-only main checkpoint again conflicted with the large shared handoff. Do **not** merge, resume, or duplicate #418/#419; #420 is the exact tested and merged implementation, intentionally excluding the concurrently edited shared handoff. This documentation-only checkpoint is committed **after** full live acceptance to avoid conflict.

### 2026-11-15 (PH) — Wallet-provider disconnect and account-change session synchronization, CI pending

**Scope:** Swap/DEX browser UI and its existing quote-state regression coverage only. Started from current remote `main` `fe1bf07893aea76902c4b8e7ae633a9286229fae` in isolated branch `codex/swap-dex-wallet-session-sync-20261115`; the shared Windows checkout and all Game, ClimbX and Launchpad paths were left untouched.

**Actual defect:** both quote-only pages retained `connectedWallet` and, potentially, a prepared order review after an external wallet disconnect or an account switch because they did not observe wallet-provider events. This could display stale wallet identity and stale review state. It does not execute a transaction today because signing/execution is source-gated off, but it is unsafe session behavior and would be misleading for the existing read-only product.

**Verified implementation commit:** `79059e4517130f7362a881a68b3d3259bf300d9a` (`fix(swap): synchronize external wallet sessions`). `swap/index.html` and `dex/index.html` now bind and remove provider `disconnect`, `accountChanged` and `accountsChanged` listeners when supported; clear the wallet identity, prepared `pendingSwapOrder` and review UI on disconnect; clear the review state and require a fresh quote on account change; and use a monotonically increasing connection epoch to ignore stale asynchronous connect results. DEX also keeps its quote and trade-terminal wallet labels synchronized and uses the established Phantom/Solflare/Backpack detection. No request route, execution flag, wallet signature, transaction submission, Durable Object, Worker or production secret was changed.

**Regression coverage:** `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.mjs` injects a synthetic event-emitting wallet for Swap and DEX at desktop and mobile viewports, verifies connect → account change → disconnect, asserts cleared review UI/listeners and asserts no `/api/swap/order` or `/api/swap/execute` request. `.github/scripts/swap-amount.test.mjs` adds source-level contracts for listener lifecycle and prepared-order cleanup. Its quote-handler extractor was repaired from LF-only to CRLF/LF matching so it continues to execute the real handler rather than failing before the assertion. This is a test correctness repair, not a weakened assertion.

### 2026-11-15 (PH) — Quote-only browser request timeout and honest unavailable recovery, CI pending

**Actual gap found during Worker/client timeout audit:** the Worker already applies a 12-second bounded Jupiter upstream request and returns a sanitized failure, but the Swap and DEX browser quote preview called `fetch()` without a client-side deadline. A browser-to-Worker connection that stalled before a response could leave the UI at `Quoting…` indefinitely even though it is strictly quote-only.

**Implementation:** commit `d78b497948552bef83cb1c8e3d8bc3a82ec7ad55` (`fix(swap): bound stalled quote requests`) adds a 15-second `AbortController` boundary to the existing quote-proxy call only in `swap/index.html` and `dex/index.html`. On client-side expiry it emits a fixed safe error, **“Climboria quote request timed out. No cached estimate was used.”** The existing catch path clears the loading control and shows unavailable state; no stale/cached price is substituted. It does not wrap or change dormant order/execute calls, Worker code, providers, signing or source gates.

**Regression coverage:** `.github/scripts/swap-amount.test.mjs` locks the timeout helper, abort signal and proxy-only quote call into both pages while continuing to execute the real Swap quote handler. `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.mjs` now aborts the quote proxy request at the browser route layer for Swap and DEX desktop/mobile, asserting an enabled recovery control, no estimate/fake cached result, no `/api/swap/order` or `/api/swap/execute`, and no page error. Local evidence: `node --test .github/scripts/swap-amount.test.mjs` **8/8 PASS**; `node --check .github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-state-smoke.mjs` PASS; both page inline-script syntax checks PASS; `git diff --check` PASS. ### 2026-11-15 PH — Provider UI close-before-load race fixed, PR #465 merged; public trade gates still CLOSED

**User request:** Continue implementing Climboria in-site third-party-provider Swap/DEX directly in GitHub. Strictly exclude Game, ClimbX, Launchpad. We have no verified current first-party Jupiter embedded terminal source/docs/SRI, and external live web verification unavailable; do not invent vendor support or enable real wallet trading.

**Security issue identified:** In `dex/provider-managed-swap.mjs`, a user could click `Close panel` while third-party provider SDK script was still loading, but its late `onload` would still call `window.Jupiter.init` and could mount a wallet-capable widget behind a hidden panel. A late async `Jupiter.init` completion could also overwrite closed-panel status. This is a **dormant** release-path hazard because `managedProvider.enabled:false` in production. Fixed **before** enabling any official vendor SDK.

**Code/PR:** [PR #465](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/465), tested head `5c74b409539caff78bd21d8472cd046d4cf634bb`, clean review and squash **merged** as [`880f88a46b0d20bd8fec190f73be176c516dc7c1`](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/commit/880f88a46b0d20bd8fec190f73be176c516dc7c1). Scope exactly 4 files: `dex/provider-managed-swap.mjs`, `.github/scripts/dex-swap-managed-provider-shell.test.mjs`, `.github/scripts/dex-swap-managed-provider-browser-smoke.mjs`, [`docs/DEX_SWAP_PROVIDER_MANAGED_CLOSE_RACE_2026-11-15.md`](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/blob/main/docs/DEX_SWAP_PROVIDER_MANAGED_CLOSE_RACE_2026-11-15.md). New `openRequestId` generation increments when new load starts and when panel closes; SDK load continuations must compare active generation and panel visibility before calling provider init. Async provider init completion/error doesn't overwrite closed panel status; reopen requires fresh explicit user click and avoids redundant SDK injection. No changes to Worker, custom signing, CPI/DO/ALT, fee config, other apps or trading activation.

**Exact PR-head tests:** [Validate 43649000431](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43649000431), [Climboria Browser Smoke 43649000476](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43649000476), [DEX Swap Read-only Quote State Regression 43649000421](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43649000421) ALL **completed SUCCESS**. The last includes successful **real Playwright Chromium synthetic-SDK SRI** tests on both `/swap/` and `/dex/`: deliberately hold mock SDK response until after user closes panel, then ensure no hidden provider `init`, explicit reopen initializes once; existing invalid-SRI, quote safety and sponsor intake tests passed. **Synthetic script tests do NOT prove real vendor compatibility.**

### 2026-11-15 PH — Current official Jupiter Plugin source verified; complete-runtime integrity blocks provider activation

**Scope:** Swap/DEX only. Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, Worker execution gates, custom `/api/swap/order` / `/api/swap/execute`, revenue settings, wallet secrets and production deployment were not changed.

**Evidence commit:** `e86500c85f2ab74cf7c63e722fa164735881db21` on `codex/swap-dex-jupiter-plugin-integrity-20261115` adds [the upstream source/integrity audit](DEX_SWAP_JUPITER_PLUGIN_SOURCE_INTEGRITY_AUDIT_2026-11-15.md). This is documentation/evidence only; it is not an enablement commit.

**Verified upstream facts:** Current official Jupiter documentation prescribes `https://plugin.jup.ag/plugin-v1.js` plus `window.Jupiter.init()` for an in-site integrated widget. The official `jup-ag/plugin` source currently reports `@jup-ag/plugin` `1.0.18`; the live versioned CDN bootstrap, application bundle and styles matched the official source commit `d5e0a6396ab38f5c5d9cae0e3763d35fc6c583bf` byte-for-byte at audit time. The full observed SHA-384 values, HTTP status/headers and legal-source links are in the audit.

**Root cause / NO-GO:** The signed bootstrap is not the complete executable chain: verified vendor source appends `plugin-1.0.18-app.js` without SRI and fetches stylesheets without integrity before injecting them into a shadow root. The public alias has only `max-age=3600`; Climboria's current prepared host is additionally tied to an obsolete `terminal.jup.ag/main-v*.js` contract. We must not enable a bootstrap-only SRI or present that as complete source protection. Provider activation remains **BLOCKED** pending a first-party complete immutable/integrity manifest (or separately reviewed all-dependency verification method), legal/compliance review, exact-artifact browser tests, and supervised owner-wallet mainnet acceptance.

**Tests/evidence performed:** direct read-only HTTPS artifact retrieval verified `200`, no redirects, CORS, byte lengths, SHA-384 values and equality with the official GitHub commit for five first-party versioned assets. No wallet, quote execution, transaction signing or mainnet transaction was attempted. Existing current-main deployment evidence remains [Pages 43650162122](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43650162122) **SUCCESS** for docs SHA `ad95122675ecb815e616e46c3f79b1efbc9e85d7`; it does not validate this branch or real trades.

**Exact next action:** Review the focused documentation PR and run its exact-head checks. Then request a first-party supported full runtime integrity/immutable-manifest mechanism from Jupiter or select another provider only after equivalent source, legal and wallet-flow verification. Preserve `managedProvider.enabled:false`, fee collection OFF, Worker execution gates OFF, and quote-only production behavior until the listed release evidence exists.

### 2026-11-16 PH — PR #509 active-panel runtime rollback shipped, postmerge production quote PASS; Jupiter staging re-synced and tested

**Safety defect and fix:** Prior first-party `dex/provider-managed-swap.mjs` already checked `/swap-runtime-status.json` on page boot, every open/reopen, and immediately before SDK init; however an ALREADY-OPEN mounted provider panel could remain interactive if the operator revoked approval without a new click. The source now polls the exact same-origin, strict, uncached release status **every 15 seconds WHILE the provider panel is open**. A revoked, unavailable, malformed or cacheable response hides the entire provider panel and launchers and stops polling. Closing manually stops polling; re-opening requires a fresh approval and starts a single new monitor. A request ID guards against a delayed FAILED status from an older closed session hiding a newer authorized session. No polling or SDK load before explicit open; no persistent polling after close.

**Security limit:** This is a **best-effort visible-UI rollback**, NOT cancellation of already displayed wallet prompts, already signed or broadcast transactions, nor a way to revoke privileges held by a loaded third-party SDK. The operator's rollback playbook must still notify active users and manage their on-chain outcomes; the periodic check may take up to its interval plus network timeout. The provider is still DISABLED on the public site and no user funds moved.

### 2026-11-16 PH — Provider tab visibility/focus/BFCache safety: merged, tested and deployed

**Scope:** Climboria **Swap and Signature DEX only**. Existing provider-managed quote-to-wallet UI has the 15-second uncached release-status poll while a third-party widget panel is open. Browsers may throttle background-tab timers, so an old, still-mounted UI could otherwise be visible on return before a new poll. [PR #518](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/518) addresses this as an additional **fail-closed exposure guard**, not a transaction cancel/rollback capability. Merged production commit: [`fa5bf9402ab3762c304db5d138e21c01750a81f9`](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/commit/fa5bf9402ab3762c304db5d138e21c01750a81f9).

**Implementation:** `dex/provider-managed-swap.mjs` registers `document.visibilitychange` (only when visible), `window.focus`, and persisted `pageshow` (BFCache return). If a provider widget is *already mounted and currently open*, it synchronously hides its panel and clears `aria-expanded` before requesting a fresh strictly no-store `/swap-runtime-status.json`; revocation, malformed response, failure, or missing no-store cache policy keeps the panel hidden and permanently hides launchers. Positive approval alone re-reveals the *same mounted UI* with one fresh 15-second interval; it does not reload the SDK or issue a transaction. Existing `openRequestId` fences out delayed responses across close/reopen, and `resumeCheckInFlight` prevents duplicate concurrent checks on focus/visibility. Manually closed panels never auto-open on tab return. **Limit:** hiding the UI does **not** terminate third-party wallet prompts, change the provider's independent permissions, roll back an already signed/submitted on-chain transaction, or make an external SDK's actions atomic with the release switch.

**Tested code scope:** PR #518 changed ONLY `dex/provider-managed-swap.mjs`, `.github/scripts/dex-swap-managed-provider-shell.test.mjs`, `.github/scripts/dex-swap-managed-provider-browser-smoke.mjs`; no Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, home/token UI, worker execution, fee, or production config source was touched. Added Node tests for synchronous concealment while approval is pending, denied tab return, allowed focus and BFCache restore without duplicate SDK init, stale approved response after manual close, and stale denied response after a newer approved reopen. Added **four real Chromium DOM scenarios** for `/swap/` and `/dex/` at 1280px desktop and 390px mobile: focus reapproval and then immediate concealment on visibility return when operator status revoked; checks never call native order/execute or a real wallet. All SDK bytes in this test are synthetic and intercepted.

**Exact PR #518 premerge CI on head `751388393b79cf7345a2f88412a65412735b9a0f`:** [DEX Swap Read-only Quote State Regression #43782720451](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782720451) **SUCCESS** (job logs explicitly contain all four new browser tab-return PASS records); [Validate Climboria #43782720500](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782720500) **SUCCESS**; [Climboria Browser Smoke Test #43782720456](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782720456) **SUCCESS**. PR file comparison verified only the three scoped Swap/DEX files; reviewed mergeability before merge.

**Exact MAIN postmerge CI on `fa5bf940...`:** [Validate #43782935063](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782935063) **SUCCESS**; [Pages build/deploy #43782934930](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782934930) **SUCCESS**; [general Browser Smoke #43782935042](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782935042) **SUCCESS**; [DEX Swap Production Quote Browser Smoke #43782935076](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782935076) **SUCCESS**; [DEX Swap Read-only Quote State Regression #43782935143](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43782935143) **SUCCESS**. ### 2026-11-16 PH — Quote preview auto-expiry + exact input display for Swap and Signature DEX (code ready for review)

**Scope:** Strictly **public read-only quote UI** in `swap/index.html` and `dex/index.html`, plus deterministic `.github/scripts/swap-amount.test.mjs` tests. Owner's two previously finalized mainnet staging transactions, screenshots and successful Cloudflare endpoint/quotes remain **completed milestones**. The official Jupiter [issue #238](https://github.com/jup-ag/plugin/issues/238) remained OPEN with zero public comments at the start of this work; existing six-hour notification watch handles any official response. The unrelated Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, homepage and tokenomics files were not touched.

**Concrete bug/reliability improvement:** The previous UI could leave a successful indicative Jupiter quote preview displaying the same monetary output indefinitely if the user did not edit tokens or amount; the warning text alone did not revoke stale figures. A user returning from a suspended/mobile background tab could still see an old quote before a throttled timeout ran. On both pages, a successfully validated **read-only** Jupiter preview now receives a **local 30-second maximum preview age** (not a claim that Jupiter protocol quote expires at 30 seconds). The pending timer is cancelled on asset/amount/slippage invalidation or when the user requests a new quote; on timeout the existing quote invalidator clears amount/output fee data and says to request a fresh quote. `visibilitychange` (visible), `focus` and `pageshow` immediately invalidate overage previews on tab return, even if browser timers were throttled. Old timers/requests are generation fenced and cannot clear a newer live quote. Both UIs also format their displayed **input amount from canonical raw u64 and token decimals** via `ClimboriaSwapAmount.formatRaw`, avoiding JavaScript `Number` round-off in the human-visible trade input.

**Deterministic test coverage added:** Existing Swap quote handler mock now supplies expiry helpers while preserving provider request assertions, and includes a full-precision `12345678901.123456789 SOL` raw-u64 display case. A new Node VM test executes each page's **actual freshness helper and invalidation source** with fake `Date.now`, fake timers and browser events; asserts fresh 29.999s preview survives focus, >30s tab return wipes stale content, normal timer wipes unchanged quotes, and generation-stale timer cannot wipe a newer preview. No wallet, RPC, signing, or real SOL required. Browser smoke and actual production acceptance are separate CI steps.

**Release truth at record-write time:** Focused source and test commits have been accepted by GitHub. PR checks, merge, real browser postmerge, and deployment are **not yet verified**. Production trade execution, managed Jupiter runtime, and Climboria platform fees intentionally remain OFF. This UI improvement is independent of waiting for official upstream dynamic JS SRI/CSS/font/distribution clearance.

**NEXT EXACT:** Open focused PR and inspect only four scoped files (Swap HTML, DEX HTML, Node tests, this master record). Verify exact-head Node/Validate/browser CI, diagnose failures and correct rather than skipping tests. ### 2026-11-16 PH — PR #563 production test failure: existing external DexScreener chart lacked sandbox; scoped corrective candidate

**Scope:** DEX/Swap UI chart iframe isolation and read-only browser/negative-control tests. No wallet execution, Worker source/config, Jupiter managed provider, fees, Game, ClimbX, Launchpad, homepage, tokenomics or owner-funded trading changes.

**Truthful failed release:** [PR #563](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/pull/563) merged as `2a524296ad8efcedffc50fba4136e663fa3be41f`, adding two *test-browser-only* telemetry-denied quote journeys while keeping the original four normal journeys. Exact PR head [static/7-control #43831926314](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43831926314) SUCCESS (browser intentionally skipped on PR) and [Validate #43831926387](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43831926387) SUCCESS. Postmerge [Pages #43832017096](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43832017096) **SUCCESS** and [Validate #43832017597](https://github.com/CryptoDilis/Climboria/actions/runs/43832017597) **SUCCESS**, but **real production browser #43832017547 FAILED**, browser job 133179424903. Its original log shows both live release-disabled probes PASS, Swap desktop raw minimum and output PASS, then **the primary unblocked Swap desktop journey failed** before any simulated analytics block: unexpected external JS requests from `pls.dexscreener.com` and `dexscreener.com` within the pre-existing external DexScreener pool chart iframe. This is **NOT** a 4/4/6/6 pass or evidence that blocking Cloudflare analytics breaks quotes. Do NOT hide the earlier failure or allowlist all DexScreener third-party JS in the top-level Climboria page.

**Verified source cause:** `swap/index.html` includes `selectedAssetChart` iframe whose src is set to DexScreener Solana pool `?embed=1` without a `sandbox` attribute; `dex/index.html` includes matching `detailChart` iframe, also without `sandbox` and previously with `referrerpolicy=origin`. The browser guard classified *all* cross-origin executable requests without accounting for the iframe's distinct browsing context. Whether the chart vendor happens to request those JS files varies by load/network; old green quote canaries do not prove they never appear.

**Corrective branch:** `fix/dex-swap-sandbox-dexscreener-charts-20261116`, from main `2a524296ad8efcedffc50fba4136e663fa3be41f`.
- `swap/index.html` commit `cca6d9845fdada67ce94933447a7fe363a92109f`: add `sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin"` to its **cross-origin chart only**, retain `no-referrer`.
- `dex/index.html` commit `e8d28eea4a59fb7a586c945ada801526e66e82ed`: same chart sandbox and reduce chart referrer from `origin` to `no-referrer`.
- `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-request-guard.mjs` commit `67d3abd492faed0168e3bf0d771f19165962675e`: pure strictly scoped frame URL guard accepts only direct-child `https://dexscreener.com/solana/<base58 pair>?embed=1` frame provenance (NOT new top-level script trust).
- `.github/scripts/dex-swap-quote-request-guard.test.mjs` commit `2d201da944e354788471d521b9e116613ffe521f`: negative controls against fake hosts, HTTP, missing `embed=1`, non-Solana, invalid pair and nested frames. Existing seven controls retained.
- `.github/scripts/dex-swap-production-quote-smoke.mjs` commit `d59acea19629dc10d153fa84fd2b896396d540c3`: separately count external executables in the expected direct-child DexScreener chart frame **only if the tested page's sole declared chart iframe has exact restrictive sandbox and no-referrer attributes**. Continue fail-closed for unknown external executable scripts/workers outside chart, all managed Jupiter wallet widget requests, known order/execute paths, Worker and same-origin API writes, both OFF runtime probes, 4 normal quotes and 2 telemetry-denied simulated quotes. Chart frame scripts remain unreviewed third-party code; sandbox restrictions do not establish their integrity.

**Verification truth:** Corrective branch code committed; focused PR, exact-head **8/8 offline controls** and Validate CI, new Pages release, and real 4 normal + 2 telemetry-denied production quote results are PENDING at this entry. Do not declare release recovery until actual **postmerge Chromium** confirms it, and fix further failures without silently broadening iframe exceptions.

**Unresolved security/release:** Public managed Jupiter runtime/native signing/execute and fee gates remain OFF. Jupiter [#238](https://github.com/jup-ag/plugin/issues/238) OPEN/0 public comments on latest check. Exact known Cloudflare analytics beacon still injected in ordinary journeys; simulated script denial does not remove it in production or approve privacy. Sandbox reduces exposure but does not substitute for a vendor audit; observe third-party chart behavior and link fallback.
